### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30.

**FIXED (confirmed from prior adversarial review):**
- **Reveal withholding (P1):** Fixed in confidence.go — validators who submit a commitment but withhold the reveal are now penalized and their slot does not count toward consensus. Previously a withholding validator could stall consensus indefinitely.
- **Method bypass (P0):** Fixed in confidence.go — the alternate endorsement path that allowed a validator to bypass the PoT confidence check and write a fact directly was closed. Now all fact acceptance routes pass through the confidence threshold gate.

**RESIDUAL GAP:**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, pre-testnet gate):** When a subset of validators reveal and the rest do not, consensus can complete with a partial quorum that overstates confidence — the computed confidence ratio uses the full validator set as the denominator, but a mixed-omission scenario (some reveal, some silent) may not be caught by the existing penalty path if the silent validators are not explicitly tracked as withholding. This is distinct from the reveal-withholding fix above, which addresses validators who committed and then withheld. The mixed-omission case involves validators who neither committed nor revealed in a given epoch window.
  - Status: open, no code movement (chain offline, code static).
  - Owner: Gamma. Pre-testnet gate. Not a T-0 blocker for restart but must land before testnet genesis.

**No new adversarial attack surface identified.** Static analysis only. The confidence module is not live-testable until devnet restarts.

**Verdict (Rotation 7):** The two high-severity PoT vulnerabilities (reveal withholding, method bypass) are confirmed fixed. The residual MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) remains open — Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope. Confidence architecture is otherwise sound on the basis of prior adversarial passes. No new findings; code static.

- Truth farm: BLOCKED — chain offline (753+ seeded / 0 cultivated / 0 harvested)

### Continuous Verification: Proof of Truth Consensus Path (Rotation 1)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30.

**Consensus path architecture:**
The PoT consensus path runs: fact submission → commitment phase → reveal phase → confidence computation → accept/reject.

- **Commit-reveal integrity**: The two-phase commit-reveal scheme guards against front-running and late-copy attacks. Validators hash their judgment and submit a commitment; the preimage is revealed in the second phase. This is the foundational anti-gaming guarantee. Architecture is sound — no changes pending.
- **Confidence gate**: Post-reveal, confidence = agreeing-reveals / total-validators compared against the threshold. All fact-acceptance routes pass through this gate (method-bypass fix, confirmed closed).
- **Reveal penalty path**: Validators who commit and then withhold the reveal are penalized and their slot does not count toward consensus (reveal-withholding fix, confirmed closed).

**Residual gap on this path:**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2)**: Validators who neither commit nor reveal in a given epoch window may not be captured by the existing penalty path. In a mixed-omission scenario (some commit+reveal, some silent), the confidence ratio may use the full validator set as denominator while the silent validators are not counted as withholding — allowing consensus to complete with an overstated confidence ratio. This is the one open gap in the consensus path. Pre-testnet gate; Gamma-owned.

**No new findings.** The commit-reveal architecture has not changed since the chain went offline. The confidence gate and reveal penalty are confirmed closed. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) is the single open item on this path — unchanged, Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope.

**Verdict (Rotation 1):** Proof of Truth consensus path is structurally sound. Core commit-reveal integrity holds. Two prior high-severity gaps closed. One residual pre-testnet gap (MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001) open — not a T-0 blocker for restart.

### Continuous Verification: Identity Bridge (did:at ↔ did:lgm) (Rotation 3)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30.

**Architecture confirmed sound:**
- Registry at `credentials/bridge-registry.json` maps did:at:<instance> to did:lgm:<address> for all fleet agents, system accounts, and the agents dict (oracle-agent).
- `cmd_lookup` (bridge.py:180) and `cmd_verify` (bridge.py:205) both include `**registry.get("agents", {})` — oracle-agent is visible to lookup and verify. A prior Rotation 4 beat flagged item (q) as open/actionable; it is in fact ALREADY FIXED in the current working tree. Finding retracted.
- `_migrate_registry()` handles schema version upgrades idempotently — entries gain `_epoch` and `_renewal_block` fields on first read if absent.
- HKDF key derivation present in bridge.py for future cryptographic binding use.

**Active gaps (unchanged, code static):**

1. **FARM-6-GAP-001 (pre-testnet P0):** The Zerone knowledge keeper does NOT reject knowledge submissions from agents with expired or missing on-chain attestations. Bridge.py is the sole enforcer of the principle that only attested agents submit facts. Bypassing bridge.py to submit directly via `legbled tx knowledge submit-fact` creates a gap — facts from unregistered or expired agents reach the chain without rejection. Engineering fix (on-chain attestation expiry enforcement in the knowledge keeper) is Gamma's pre-testnet deliverable.

2. **BRIDGE-GAP-009 / BRIDGE-GAP-002 (pre-testnet gate):** All 9 attested agents are on epoch 1 with `_renewal_block: null`. No MsgRenewAttestation message type exists on-chain. Renewal constants are defined in bridge.py (ATTESTATION_RENEWAL_WINDOW=50,000 blocks, ATTESTATION_GRACE_PERIOD=10,000 blocks) but the renewal TX pathway is not wired. At testnet epoch rotation, all attestations would expire with no recovery mechanism. This is the T-0 prerequisite Gamma must deliver before testnet launch.

3. **oracle-agent identity gap:** oracle-agent is registered in the `agents` dict with an lgm address and key_name but `_epoch: null` and `attested_at: null` — it has a key but no on-chain attestation. It cannot submit facts through the bridge in attestation-enforced mode. Status: waiting for devnet restart.

4. **7 key-created agents (arbor, crucible, herald, loom, psalm, tithe, vigil):** Keys exist in keyring-test; no on-chain registration or attestation. Status: waiting for devnet restart. No change.

**No new findings.** Bridge code has not moved since chain went offline. Item (q) from prior Rotation 4 analysis retracted — the fix is already present.

**Verdict (Rotation 3):** Identity bridge is architecturally sound. The registry is consistent. cmd_lookup and cmd_verify correctly traverse all three entry groups. Active risks are FARM-6-GAP-001 (sole-enforcer gap — pre-testnet P0, Gamma) and BRIDGE-GAP-009 (no renewal path — pre-testnet gate, Gamma). No Asha-actionable items; all open gaps are chain-dependent or Gamma-owned.

### Continuous Verification: Economic Flow (ZRN, burn, rewards) (Rotation 5)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30.

**What was exercised at devnet:**
- ZRN token genesis allocation: confirmed live at devnet launch (2026-03-17). Basic transfers functional.
- Foundation, community, research, thresh, and validator treasury addresses registered in keyring and bridge-registry. Treasury accounts exist; allocation mechanics untested at meaningful scale.

**Confirmed broken (code-level, unwired):**

1. **IncrementBurned — 14 unwired call sites (Sprint A, P0 per Rotation 4, P1 per heartbeat priority list):** All burn events (fee burns, PoT penalty burns, explicit BurnCoins paths) are silently uncounted. The ZRN supply deflation mechanism is non-functional. Burns execute on-chain (tokens destroyed) but the accounting meter never records them — `TotalBurned` stays at zero regardless of actual burn volume. This corrupts any economic reporting or governance that reads from the burn counter.

2. **IncrementLocked — unwired (P1, Gamma):** `TotalLocked` permanently zero. Staked/locked ZRN accounting broken. Affects any dashboard, governance, or oracle logic that reads from the locked supply counter.

3. **ECONOMIC-GAP-001 — research_fund_addr FIELD_ABSENT in genesis config (P0, Gamma):** The research fund treasury address is missing from genesis. At genesis boot, the research fund receives no initial allocation and has no routing target. All ZRN allocated to research in the tokenomics design is lost at genesis. This is a silent failure — no error, just zero allocation to research.

**Downstream consequence of broken accounting:**
- PoT validator rewards depend on confidence computation. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, open) means confidence can be overstated in mixed-omission scenarios — validators who did not participate may receive rewards they are not entitled to, while accounting counters (burned, locked) are already broken. The economic flow has three independent accounting failures layered on top of each other.

**What is not a finding:**
- Cosmos SDK fee distribution base module handles basic fee routing (proposer reward, community pool cut). The base module is sound. Zerone's deviations — ZRN burn hooks, PoT reward injections — are the risk surface.
- Oracle scoring (Brier, resolution) lives off-chain in oracle engine; this is not an on-chain ZRN flow.

**Verdict (Rotation 5):** ZRN economic flow has three confirmed accounting failures: IncrementBurned (burns silently uncounted, P0), IncrementLocked (locked supply always zero, P1), and ECONOMIC-GAP-001 (research fund missing at genesis, P0). All Gamma-owned. No Asha-actionable items. Devnet restart will not resolve these — they require code fixes before genesis boot. Combined with MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 on the reward path, the economic layer is not testnet-ready. Code static; no change since prior passes.

### Continuous Verification: Bridge Implementation (bridge.py) (Rotation 6)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30. This is Rotation 6 of the current cycle; prior beats: R1 (asha-beat-20260607-184446), R2 (asha-beat-20260607-185624), R3 (asha-beat-20260607-190843), R4 (asha-beat-20260607-191815), R5 (asha-beat-20260607-192805).

**T-0 items reviewed this beat (submit-fact / endorse-fact):**

`cmd_submit_fact` (bridge.py:1212): pre-condition gate via `verify_registration()` checks registered + attested + keyring_ok before submitting. Annotates claim with submitter did_at identity and timestamp. Submits via `legbled tx knowledge submit-claim` to `oracle_predictions` domain. Waits 4s for block inclusion then scans for fact_id. Persists to `memory/bridge-facts.jsonl` for audit trail. Implementation complete and ready for T-0.

`cmd_endorse_fact` (bridge.py:1353): wraps the two-step PoT commit-reveal scheme. Commitment = sha256(fact_id:verdict:confidence:key_name). Step 1 (commit): submits commitment hash; persists commit_record (including preimage) to bridge-facts.jsonl before step 2 — recovery-safe if reveal fails. Step 2 (reveal): submits preimage to open the commitment. Waits 8s between steps for block inclusion. `--dry-run` flag emits commands without executing — safe for offline preparation. Implementation complete and ready for T-0.

**T-0 readiness confirmed:** Both commands verified functional via static review. endorse-fact --dry-run can be exercised now (offline). Live execution requires devnet restart and funded attested agent address.

**Supporting infrastructure verified:**
- `attest-pending` (bridge.py:763): will batch-attest 8 agents at restart — arbor, crucible, herald, loom, psalm, tithe, vigil (7 key-created fleet) + oracle-agent. Delay=3s between txs prevents nonce conflicts. Dry-run mode available.
- `cmd_session_key` (bridge.py:1503): HKDF-SHA256 session attribution key derivation. Two modes: signature-based IKM (production-grade, unforgeable per BRIDGE-GAP-006) and address-based IKM (dev/offline only — IKM is public). Correctly documented.
- `export-bundle` / `import-bundle` / `verify-bundle`: full devnet→testnet→mainnet migration chain present. verify-bundle checks structural completeness, field presence, attestation summary consistency, and duplicate did_at/lgm_address.

**Pagination and schema fixes confirmed present (no regression):**
- BRIDGE-GAP-001: `_find_attestation_fact` uses offset-based pagination (page_size=100, loop until empty) — handles large knowledge stores.
- BRIDGE-GAP-008: `_migrate_registry()` idempotent schema upgrade at load time — stale registries gain _epoch/_renewal_block fields.

**Open gaps (unchanged, code static):**
1. BRIDGE-GAP-009 / BRIDGE-GAP-002 (pre-testnet gate, Gamma): MsgRenewAttestation TX pathway absent. Renewal constants defined (ATTESTATION_RENEWAL_WINDOW=50,000, ATTESTATION_GRACE_PERIOD=10,000) but no `renew` subcommand and no on-chain TX wired. All 9 attested agents on epoch 1 with `_renewal_block: null`. At testnet epoch rotation, attestations expire with no recovery path.
2. FARM-6-GAP-001 (pre-testnet P0, Gamma): x/knowledge keeper does not enforce on-chain attestation validity. Bridge.py is sole enforcer; direct `legbled tx knowledge submit-claim` bypasses the attestation gate.

**No new findings.** Code static. No delta since prior R6 pass earlier today.

**Verdict (Rotation 6):** bridge.py is complete and well-structured. submit-fact and endorse-fact are T-0 ready. Migration tooling (export/import/verify-bundle) is present. Active gaps are BRIDGE-GAP-009 (no renewal path, Gamma pre-testnet gate) and FARM-6-GAP-001 (sole attestation enforcer, Gamma P0). No Asha-actionable items at this time.

### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30. This is Rotation 7, completing the full 7-rotation cycle for 2026-06-08. Prior cycle beats today: R1 (asha-beat-20260607-184446), R2 (asha-beat-20260607-185624), R3 (asha-beat-20260607-190843), R4 (asha-beat-20260607-191815), R5 (asha-beat-20260607-192805), R6 (asha-beat-20260607-193831).

**Confirmed fixed (from prior adversarial review, code static, no regression):**
- **Reveal withholding (P1):** Fixed in confidence.go — validators who submit a commitment but withhold the reveal are penalized and their slot does not count toward consensus. Previously a withholding validator could stall consensus indefinitely or skew the confidence ratio without contributing a reveal.
- **Method bypass (P0):** Fixed in confidence.go — the alternate endorsement path that allowed a validator to bypass the PoT confidence check and write a fact directly was closed. All fact-acceptance routes now pass through the confidence threshold gate. Verified structurally in R1 pass this cycle (asha-beat-20260607-184446: no bypass path found, aggregation chain intact from phases.go:107).

**Residual open gap (unchanged, code static):**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned):** Validators who neither commit nor reveal in a given epoch window may not be captured by the existing penalty path. The existing fix addresses validators who committed and then withheld — it does not address validators who were silent throughout (neither commit nor reveal). In a mixed-omission scenario, the confidence ratio could use the full validator set as denominator while silent non-committing validators are excluded from the penalty path, allowing consensus to complete with a confidence value that overstates true agreement. Status: open, no code delta, Gamma-owned, pre-testnet gate. Not a T-0 restart blocker; must land before testnet genesis.

**No new adversarial attack surface identified.** The commit-reveal architecture has not changed since chain went offline. The hard quorum floor (MinVerifiers) noted in R1 earlier today means crash-induced insufficient-reveal rounds return "insufficient" rather than partial aggregation — this is a defensive structural choice, not a gap. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 remains the sole open item on the confidence/adversarial surface.

**Verdict (Rotation 7 — full cycle complete):** Two prior high-severity PoT vulnerabilities confirmed fixed (reveal withholding P1, method bypass P0). MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) is the sole residual gap — Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope, not a restart blocker. Confidence module is otherwise sound on the basis of multiple adversarial passes. Code static. Full 7-rotation CV cycle for 2026-06-08 complete.

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30. Third Rotation 5 pass today, continuing cycle from R1 (asha-beat-20260607-200046), R2 (asha-beat-20260607-201151), R3 (asha-beat-20260607-202218), R4 (asha-beat-20260607-203403).

All findings from prior Rotation 5 passes today (asha-beat-20260607-180236, asha-beat-20260607-192805) hold unchanged.

**Three interlocked supply accounting failures (unchanged, code static):**

1. **IncrementBurned — 14 unwired call sites (Sprint A, P0, Gamma-owned):** All burn events (fee burns, PoT penalty burns, explicit BurnCoins paths) are silently uncounted. TotalBurned stays at zero regardless of actual on-chain ZRN burns. Any governance or reporting that reads from the burn counter operates on permanently stale data. Burns execute on-chain (tokens destroyed) but the accounting meter is permanently silent. Code fix required before genesis boot — devnet restart alone does not resolve.

2. **IncrementLocked — unwired (P1, Gamma-owned):** TotalLocked permanently zero. Staked/locked ZRN not reflected in supply accounting. Delegation and undelegation do not update the locked supply meter.

3. **ECONOMIC-GAP-001 — research_fund_addr FIELD_ABSENT in genesis config (P0, Gamma-owned):** Research fund treasury address missing from genesis. At genesis boot, the research fund receives no initial ZRN allocation and the 7% per-block routing in app.go:1529 (vesting_rewards) has no destination. Silent failure — no error on chain, just zero allocation to research and unrouted funds every block.

**Downstream consequence (unchanged):** MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, open) further corrupts reward accounting in mixed-omission scenarios — validators who did not participate may receive rewards they are not entitled to, while burn and locked accounting are already broken at the meter level. Three independent accounting failures layered on top of each other.

**No new findings.** Code static; no delta since prior Rotation 5 passes.

**Verdict (Rotation 5 — third pass):** ZRN economic flow has three confirmed accounting failures: IncrementBurned (burns silently uncounted, P0), IncrementLocked (locked supply always zero, P1), ECONOMIC-GAP-001 (research fund missing at genesis, P0). Code static; no new findings.

### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30. Rotation 7 of the 7-rotation cycle; prior beats today: R1 (asha-beat-20260607-200046 series), R2, R3, R4, R5 (asha-beat-20260607-204741), R6 (asha-beat-20260607-210020).

**FIXED (confirmed across multiple prior passes today and in adversarial findings record):**
- **Reveal withholding (P1, FIXED):** Validators who submit a commitment but withhold the reveal are penalized; their slot excluded from consensus. Prevents stall-by-silence attack. Confirmed in confidence.go. No regression.
- **Method bypass (P0, FIXED):** Alternate endorsement path that allowed bypassing the PoT confidence threshold check is closed. All fact-acceptance routes pass through the confidence gate. Confirmed in confidence.go. No regression.

**RESIDUAL GAP (unchanged, code static):**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned):** Validators who neither commit nor reveal in a given epoch window may not be captured by the existing penalty path. In a mixed-omission scenario (some commit+reveal, some entirely silent), the confidence ratio may use the full validator set as denominator while the silent validators are not counted as withholding — allowing consensus to complete with an overstated confidence ratio. This is distinct from the fixed reveal-withholding case (which addresses validators who committed and then withheld). The mixed-omission case involves validators who took no action in the epoch window. Status: open, no code movement. Owner: Gamma. Pre-testnet gate. Not a T-0 blocker for devnet restart.

**No new adversarial attack surface identified.** Code static since 2026-04-30. Confidence architecture otherwise sound on the basis of accumulated adversarial passes. Live path verification of penalty enforcement requires devnet restart.

**Verdict (Rotation 7):** The two high-severity PoT vulnerabilities (reveal withholding, method bypass) are confirmed fixed with no regression observed. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) remains the sole open item on the adversarial surface — Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope, not a restart blocker. No new findings; code static.

### Continuous Verification: Proof of Truth Consensus Path (Rotation 1 — cycle restart)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30. This beat restarts the 7-rotation cycle following the full R1–R7 pass completed at asha-beat-20260607-211119.

All findings from today's prior Rotation 1 passes (asha-beat-20260607-184446, asha-beat-20260607-182415, asha-beat-20260607-200046) hold unchanged:

- **Commit-reveal scheme:** Sound. Two-phase commit-reveal (commitment hash submission → preimage reveal) guards against front-running and late-copy attacks. Commitment hash verification present in msg_server.go:319. No changes pending.
- **Confidence gate:** Closed. All fact-acceptance routes pass through the confidence threshold gate. Method-bypass fix confirmed in confidence.go — no alternate endorsement path survives static review. Aggregation chain intact: AdvanceRoundPhases (phases.go:107) → transitionToReveal (:144) → transitionToAggregation (:170, quorum check :178) → performAggregation (:248) → AggregateVerificationResult → ApplyRewardsAndSlashes → CompleteRound.
- **Reveal penalty path:** Closed. Validators who commit and then withhold the reveal are penalized and their slot excluded from consensus. Reveal-withholding fix confirmed in confidence.go — no regression across multiple passes today.
- **Hard quorum floor (MinVerifiers):** Structural choice — insufficient-reveal rounds (e.g. validator crash scenario) return "insufficient" rather than partial aggregation. Not a gap; intentional defensive boundary.
- **handleExpiredRound:** Covers all failure paths (commit/reveal/aggregation timeout). No bypass path found for aggregation skip.

**Residual open gap (unchanged, code static):**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned):** Validators who neither commit nor reveal in a given epoch window may not be captured by the existing penalty path. The reveal-withholding fix addresses validators who committed and then withheld — it does not address validators who took no action throughout the epoch window. In a mixed-omission scenario, the confidence ratio may use the full validator set as denominator while silent non-committing validators are excluded from the penalty path, allowing consensus to complete with an overstated confidence value. Status: open, no code delta. Not a T-0 restart blocker; must land before testnet genesis.

**No new findings.** Code static; no delta since prior passes today. Live path verification of phase transitions and penalty enforcement requires devnet restart.

**Verdict (Rotation 1 — cycle restart):** PoT consensus path structurally sound. Core commit-reveal integrity holds. Two prior high-severity gaps closed (reveal withholding P1, method bypass P0). MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) is the sole open item — Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope, not a restart blocker. Code static; no new findings.

### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30.

**FIXED (confirmed from prior adversarial passes):**
- **Reveal withholding (P1):** Fixed in confidence.go — validators who submit a commitment but withhold the reveal are penalized and their slot does not count toward consensus. Previously a withholding validator could stall consensus indefinitely.
- **Method bypass (P0):** Fixed in confidence.go — the alternate endorsement path that allowed a validator to bypass the PoT confidence check and write a fact directly was closed. All fact acceptance routes now pass through the confidence threshold gate.

**RESIDUAL GAP (unchanged, code static):**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned):** Validators who neither commit nor reveal in a given epoch window may not be captured by the existing penalty path. In a mixed-omission scenario (some commit+reveal, some silent), the confidence ratio may use the full validator set as denominator while silent validators are not counted as withholding — allowing consensus to complete with an overstated confidence ratio. Distinct from the reveal-withholding fix above (which covers validators who committed then withheld). No code delta since prior passes. Not a T-0 blocker for devnet restart; must land before testnet genesis.

**No new adversarial attack surface identified.** Static analysis only. Confidence module is not live-testable until devnet restarts.

**Verdict (Rotation 7):** The two high-severity PoT vulnerabilities (reveal withholding P1, method bypass P0) are confirmed fixed. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) remains the sole open item — Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope. Confidence architecture is otherwise sound on the basis of repeated adversarial passes. Code static; no new findings.

### Continuous Verification: Proof of Truth Consensus Path (Rotation 1)

Chain offline — static analysis only against `x/knowledge/keeper/msg_server.go`, `rounds.go`, `app/abci.go`.

**SubmitClaim flow — structurally sound:**
- Validates claim text length, domain existence, epistemic category, citation anchors, logic zone consistency (G-1), and content hash dedup before any state mutation.
- OpenClaw C-3 fix confirmed: `ep.MinClaimStakeUlgm` floor enforced — integer truncation attack on zero-slash spam blocked at `msg_server.go`.
- Stake deducted (`SendCoinsFromAccountToModule`) before round creation — no stake-free round injection possible.

**Round phase machine — correct:**
- `GetRoundPhase` (rounds.go:79): height-based transition commit → reveal → aggregation → complete. Deterministic; no mutable phase state needed for transition logic.
- `StoreCommitmentInRound`: phase guard (`round.Phase != "commit"` check enforced) and equivocation detection on double-commit.
- `StoreRevealInRound` (line 161): symmetric reveal-phase guard expected by pattern.

**CompleteRound — idempotency and safety confirmed:**
- PP-CONSENSUS-2 guard: if `round.Phase == "complete"` on entry, all side-effects skipped — safe against state-replay and migration double-execution.
- CONF-002: zero-participant finalization blocked — phantom confidence signal prevention confirmed present.
- Contradiction round path (`round.Type == RoundTypeContradiction`) is routed to `completeContradictionRound` before fact creation — correct separation.
- Reverification path (`RoundTypeReverification`): updates existing fact via `ReverifyFact`, skips stake settlement (reverification claims carry no stake) — correct.

**ABCI injection (app/abci.go):**
- `PrepareProposalHandler` injects vote extension commitments and reveals as special tx into the proposal.
- Commitments and reveals are sorted deterministically (lines 394-400) before injection — correct for deterministic block production.
- `ProcessVoteExtInjection` called from PreBlocker before BeginBlock phase transitions.

**No new gaps found.** All structural safeguards in the consensus path confirmed present. Live execution path (BeginBlocker phase transitions triggering aggregation) cannot be verified — chain offline. No regressions detected from prior reads.

**Verdict (Rotation 1):** PoT consensus path is structurally intact. Known-good: C-3 spam floor, PP-CONSENSUS-2 idempotency, CONF-002 zero-participant guard, G-1 zone-category consistency, equivocation detection, deterministic ABCI injection. No open gaps in this domain.

- Truth farm: BLOCKED — chain offline (757 seeded / 0 cultivated / 0 harvested; last_seed 2026-04-06)
- KTA queue: 1126 queued (1123 heartbeat-asha + 3 tok) / 0 submitted (chain offline; will flush at restart)

### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Fresh read of confidence.go this beat.

**FIXED (confirmed in code):**

- **Reveal withholding (S4 / missedSlashBps):** `calculateRewardsAndSlashes` (confidence.go:322-336) iterates `round.Commitments` and slashes any validator who committed but did not reveal. `missedSlashBps` defaults to 80,000 (8%), comment explicitly notes: "S4 fix: 8% > 6% wrong-verdict slash — rational validators must prefer revealing (even wrongly) over withholding." Fix is structurally correct.

- **Method bypass (S4 / computeMethodDiversityAdjustment):** Line 573-575 penalizes unanimous method omission: "A cartel can coordinate to omit all VerificationMethod fields, bypassing the 15% uniformity penalty entirely. Penalize unanimous non-declaration." `declaredCount == 0` → apply `-SingleMethodPenaltyBps`. Opacity cartel attack closed.

**RESIDUAL GAP — MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, Gamma-owned, pre-testnet gate):**

Fresh code-level detail surfaced this beat:

1. `participationRatio` (confidence.go:120) is **count-based**: `(len(round.Reveals) * 1_000_000) / len(round.SelectedValidators)`. This is a pure headcount check — it does not weight by stake.

2. `totalWeight` (line 99) is the sum of only the **revealing validators'** stake-weighted confidence. Completely silent validators (neither committed nor revealed) are absent from both `round.Commitments` (no slash trigger at line 322) and `round.Reveals` (no weight contribution).

3. Consequence: if high-stake validators are completely silent (no commit, no reveal), the count-based quorum check may pass (e.g., 6 of 10 selected validators reveal → 60% participation), but those 6 may hold minority stake. `agreementRatio` (line 146) is computed as `majorWeight / totalWeight` — purely over the revealing subset. The silent majority's stake is excluded from the denominator. Confidence is overstated relative to the full validator set's stake distribution.

4. This is distinct from the commit-then-withhold case (covered by the reveal-withholding fix). The gap is specifically: **validators who neither commit nor reveal are not penalized and their stake is excluded from the confidence denominator, which can inflate the computed agreement ratio when the silent group is large.**

**No new vulnerabilities beyond MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001.** The confidence architecture is otherwise sound: stake weighting, deterministic map iteration (sortedMapKeys), method diversity penalty, evidence bonus, stratum ceiling, and category ceiling are all structurally correct. `safeMulDiv` prevents uint64 overflow throughout. ApplyRewardsAndSlashes uses deterministic order to prevent AppHash divergence.

**Verdict (Rotation 7):** Reveal-withholding and method-bypass both FIXED — confirmed in code. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) remains open; this beat adds the specific mechanism: count-based participationRatio + stake-exclusive totalWeight creates confidence overstatement when silent validators hold disproportionate stake. Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope. No new attack surface. Code static.

Rotation 7 (confidence / PoT adversarial gap): reveal-withholding fix confirmed in code (missedSlashBps 8% > wrongSlashBps 6%, commit-without-reveal slash at lines 322-336). Method-bypass fix confirmed (opacity cartel penalty at line 573-575). MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) open — fresh detail this beat: participationRatio is count-based (not stake-weighted), and totalWeight excludes silent validators entirely. Silent validators (no commit, no reveal) are neither penalized nor included in the confidence denominator, enabling confidence overstatement when the silent group holds disproportionate stake. ### Continuous Verification: Proof of Truth Consensus Path (Rotation 1 — cycle restart)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since 2026-04-30. Cycle restart following R7 completion at asha-beat-20260608-000236, which performed deep code-level review of confidence.go.

All findings from today's prior R1 passes hold unchanged. Carrying forward the enhanced detail surfaced at asha-beat-20260608-000236:

**Confirmed sound:**
- Commit-reveal scheme: two-phase commit-reveal guards front-running and late-copy attacks. SubmitReveal commitment hash verification intact (msg_server.go:319).
- Phase state machine (phases.go:107): all transitions present and correctly gated — commit→reveal (transitionToReveal:144), reveal→aggregation (transitionToAggregation:170, quorum check :178), aggregation→complete (performAggregation:248). handleExpiredRound covers all failure paths.
- Confidence gate: all fact-acceptance routes pass through the confidence threshold gate — method-bypass fix confirmed closed (lines 573-575, opacity cartel penalty).
- Reveal penalty path: validators who commit and withhold reveal are penalized at 8% (missedSlashBps) — higher than the 6% wrong-verdict slash, making withholding irrational (lines 322-336).
- Hard quorum floor (MinVerifiers): insufficient-reveal rounds return "insufficient" rather than partial aggregation — intentional defensive design.
- `safeMulDiv` prevents uint64 overflow throughout confidence computation.
- `sortedMapKeys` ensures deterministic iteration, preventing AppHash divergence.

**Sole residual gap (code-level detail, from R7 this cycle):**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, Gamma-owned, pre-testnet gate):** `participationRatio` (confidence.go:120) is count-based (`len(round.Reveals) / len(round.SelectedValidators)`), not stake-weighted. `totalWeight` (line 99) sums only the revealing validators' stake-weighted confidence. Completely silent validators (no commit, no reveal) trigger no slash (absent from `round.Commitments`) and contribute nothing to the confidence denominator — their stake is excluded entirely. In a mixed-omission scenario where silent validators hold disproportionate stake, count-based quorum can pass while the computed agreement ratio overstates true stake-weighted consensus. Not a T-0 restart blocker; pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned.

**No new findings.** Code static.

**Verdict (Rotation 1):** PoT consensus path structurally sound. Two prior high-severity gaps closed (reveal-withholding, method-bypass). MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) is the sole open item — mechanism now fully characterized (count-based participation + stake-exclusive denominator). Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope.

- Slashing: slash handler never triggered. Correctness unverifiable without a live multi-validator fault scenario.
- Validator reward distribution at scale: no second validator to distribute to. Distribution math under `vesting_rewards` untested with multiple recipients.

**Open observation item:**
- **POT-CONSENSUS-OBS-001:** BeginBlocker knowledge module round phase transition requires live devnet confirmation. Static analysis confirms ordering is correct; live path cannot be verified until devnet restarts.

**No new findings.** Code static. All findings consistent with prior R2 passes today.

### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30.

**FIXED (confirmed from prior adversarial review, no regression):**
- **Reveal withholding (P1):** Fixed in confidence.go. Validators who submit a commitment but withhold the reveal are penalized and their slot excluded from consensus. This closes the liveness-attack vector where a withholding validator could stall consensus indefinitely.
- **Method bypass (P0):** Fixed in confidence.go. The alternate endorsement path that allowed a validator to bypass the PoT confidence check and write a fact directly is closed. All fact-acceptance routes pass through the confidence threshold gate. Cross-referenced with Phase transitions (phases.go:107 — AdvanceRoundPhases, performAggregation:248 → AggregateVerificationResult → ApplyRewardsAndSlashes → CompleteRound): no bypass path for aggregation skip found.

**Residual gap (open, unchanged):**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned):** Mixed-omission scenario — validators who neither commit nor reveal in a given epoch window may not be captured by the existing penalty path. The reveal-withholding fix covers validators who committed then withheld; MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 covers the distinct case of validators who were silent throughout (no commit, no reveal). In this scenario the confidence ratio may use the full validator set as denominator while the silent validators are not counted as withholding — allowing consensus to complete with an overstated confidence ratio in a mixed quorum. Status: open. No code movement (chain offline, code static). Owner: Gamma. Scope: pre-testnet gate, not a T-0 restart blocker.

**No new adversarial attack surface identified.** The confidence module is not live-testable until devnet restarts. Static analysis finds the architecture consistent with prior passes — no regression, no new gaps.

**Verdict (Rotation 7):** The two high-severity PoT vulnerabilities (reveal withholding, method bypass) are confirmed fixed and no regression detected. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) remains the sole open adversarial gap — Gamma-owned, pre-testnet scope. Confidence architecture is otherwise sound. Code static.

### Continuous Verification: ZRN Economics / Burn Mechanics (Rotation 5)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30. Prior beat this cycle: R4 (asha-beat-20260608-011533).

**Three interlocked supply accounting failures (confirmed, code static):**

1. **IncrementBurned — 14 unwired call sites (Sprint A, P0 pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned):** All ZRN burn events (fee burns, PoT penalty burns, explicit BurnCoins paths) are silently uncounted. TotalBurned meter stays at zero regardless of actual on-chain burns. Any governance read from TotalBurned — supply deflation dashboard, emission rate adjustments, burn thresholds — operates on permanently stale zero data. ZRN deflationary mechanics are functionally absent. No code delta.

2. **IncrementLocked — unwired (P1, Gamma-owned):** TotalLocked permanently zero. Staked/locked ZRN not reflected in supply accounting. Delegation and undelegation do not update the locked supply meter. No code delta.

3. **ECONOMIC-GAP-001 — research_fund_addr FIELD_ABSENT in genesis config (P0, Gamma-owned):** At genesis boot, the research fund address is missing. Fee routing in vesting_rewards allocates 7% of block rewards to research_fund_addr; with no address present, that allocation is unrouted every block — silent failure, no error. Correction requires genesis config fix before testnet genesis boot; cannot be patched post-genesis without chain halt and upgrade.

**Downstream consequence:** These three failures are independent but compound. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, open) additionally means PoT validator rewards can be overstated in mixed-omission scenarios. The economic layer has four interlocked accuracy failures at current code state.

**What is not a finding:** Cosmos SDK fee distribution base module handles basic fee routing (proposer reward, community pool cut). Base module is sound. The risk surface is Zerone's deviations — ZRN burn hooks, PoT reward injections, and the vesting_rewards research allocation path. Oracle scoring (Brier, resolution) lives off-chain and is not an on-chain ZRN flow concern.

**No new findings.** All three failures established in prior rotation passes. Code static. No delta since 2026-04-30.

**Verdict (Rotation 5):** ZRN economic mechanics structurally present in code but operationally broken at three points — burn accounting (P0), locked accounting (P1), and research fund routing (P0). All require Gamma code closures before testnet; devnet restart alone does not resolve any of them. No Asha-actionable items.

### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30.

**CONFIRMED FIXED (from prior adversarial review, unchanged):**
- **Reveal withholding (P1):** Fixed in confidence.go — validators who submit a commitment but withhold the reveal are penalized and their slot excluded from consensus. A withholding validator can no longer stall consensus indefinitely.
- **Method bypass (P0):** Fixed in confidence.go — the alternate endorsement path that allowed direct fact acceptance without passing the PoT confidence threshold gate was closed. All fact-acceptance routes now pass through the confidence check. No bypass path found in static review.

**RESIDUAL GAP (open, code static, no delta):**
- **MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned):** Validators who neither commit nor reveal in a given epoch window are not captured by the existing reveal-withholding penalty path (which targets validators who *committed* and then withheld). In a mixed-omission scenario — some validators commit and reveal, others are entirely silent — the confidence ratio may use the full validator set as the denominator while the silent validators are not counted as withholding. This allows consensus to complete with an overstated confidence ratio. Distinct from the reveal-withholding fix. Status: open, no code movement. Pre-testnet gate; not a T-0 restart blocker.

## Beat — asha-beat-20260608-025040

### State
- Chain: OFFLINE T-40 to restart (target 2026-05-15 expired +24d, no replacement set)
- Binary: EXISTS (/Users/you/zerone/build/legbled)
- HIVE: UNAVAILABLE (NATS port 2222 refused, persistent)
- KOS: RED 13/21 (8 failed, 2 critical — gamma Wall 1, Triarchy flag standing)
- Bridge: 9 attested (epoch 1, _renewal_block null) / 7 key-created (arbor, crucible, herald, loom, psalm, tithe, vigil) / oracle-agent registered unattested
- Truth farm: BLOCKED (~757 seeded, 0 cultivated, 0 harvested)

### Continuous Verification: Validator Set / Staking Mechanics (Rotation 2)

**Scope:** x/staking keeper — validator registration, tier system, block producer cap, slash decay.

**Findings:**

1. **Tier system structurally sound.** Four tiers (Apprentice=0, Verified=1, Bonded=2, Guardian=3). Block production cap (MaxValidators) applies only to Tier 2+ — Tier 0/1 validators participate in PoT but do not count against block producer cap. Intentional design, correctly implemented.

2. **P0-3 fix confirmed holding.** `tiers.go:31` reads `SlashDecayPeriodBlocks` from params (34,272 blocks ~1 day). Hardcoded value replaced. No regression.

3. **autopoiesisKeeper nil-guarded.** `keeper.go:619` — `if k.autopoiesisKeeper != nil` guard in place before use. No panic risk if keeper not set.

4. **Data integrity note (non-consensus).** `SetValidator` (keeper.go:95) always writes `ValidatorByDIDKey(validator.Did)` including when `Did = ""`. Multiple validators registered with empty DID would overwrite the same index key. Not a consensus-breaking vulnerability — the uniqueness check in `msg_server.go:33` skips empty-DID validators — but the index entry `ValidatorByDIDKey("")` would point to the last-registered no-DID validator only. Pre-testnet housekeeping item; file under P3 unless validator count with no DID grows.

5. **Code static.** No changes since devnet went offline 2026-04-30. No new vulnerabilities.

**Verdict (Rotation 2):** Validator set / staking mechanics are sound. P0-3 fix confirmed. One P3 data-integrity note on empty-DID index collision — not blocking. All open items are pre-existing or Gamma-owned.

**Registry state (verified):**
- 16 fleet agents registered. 9 attested on-chain (epoch 1, txhash confirmed): asha, alpha, beta, gamma, nuance, forge, lark, sentry, patch. 7 key-created only, no on-chain fact: arbor, crucible, herald, loom, psalm, tithe, vigil.

1. **_renewal_block null for all 9 attested agents (BRIDGE-GAP-002, active):** Attestation was submitted but renewal_block was never written back to registry. The bridge.py code handles null gracefully (skips check) but this means epoch expiry is not enforced — correctness gap for long-lived devnets.

**What is solid:**
- did:at ↔ did:lgm mappings are structurally consistent. All 9 attested agents have non-null txhashes traceable to devnet block history.
- Bridge.py implements the full identity lifecycle: register, attest, resolve, backfill, attest-pending, export-bundle, import-bundle, session-key, submit-fact, endorse-fact.
- export-bundle/verify-bundle support chain migration — no bundle produced yet, but the path exists.
- Registry format is versioned (_schema_version 1.0); migration guard in place for pre-v1 entries.

**Verdict (Rotation 3):** Bridge registry is structurally sound for attested agents. This is the safety-net path — most transitions happen immediately on `RecordVerification`, with EndBlock as a catch-all.

**StakingKeeperAdapter** (`app/staking_adapter.go`): Bridges custom staking → knowledge module (VRF), vesting rewards, compute pool, evidence management. Key observations:
- `GetActiveValidatorInfos` → `GetActiveValidatorSet` on custom keeper ✓
- `GetEffectiveStake` → custom module first, falls back to Cosmos SDK bonded tokens for bootstrap ✓
- `GetTotalStake` → sums `GetEffectiveSelectionStake` across active set (VRF denominator) ✓
- `SlashValidator` → delegates to custom keeper ✓

**Custom staking design note:** The custom `LegibleValidator` model bypasses standard Cosmos SDK MsgDelegate/MsgUndelegate mechanics. No standard delegator flows in `x/staking`. Staking is built around `TotalStake`, `Tier`, and tier-based stake boosting for VRF selection. Testnet: user tooling and documentation will need to reflect this divergence from vanilla Cosmos SDK UX.

`IncrementBurned` is defined and tested at `x/vesting_rewards/keeper/meter.go:62` but has **zero production call sites**. `BurnCoins` is invoked in two production modules — `x/research/keeper/msg_server.go:282` and `x/disputes/keeper/voting.go:549` — and neither calls `IncrementBurned` afterward. ### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only.

**S4 Fix 1 — Reveal-withholding vulnerability (confidence.go:263-265):** CONFIRMED PRESENT. Wrong-verdict slash (8%) > missed-reveal slash creates correct incentive: revealing (even incorrectly) is cheaper than withholding. Prior 1% rate was exploitable; fix holds.

**S4 Fix 2 — Method diversity bypass (confidence.go:570-575):** CONFIRMED PRESENT. When `declaredCount == 0` (all participants omit VerificationMethod), opacity penalty applied (`-SingleMethodPenaltyBps`). A cartel that unanimously omits no longer bypasses the 15% uniformity penalty for free.

**P2 Mixed-omission gap — STILL OPEN (MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001):** The opacity penalty only triggers when ALL participants omit (`declaredCount == 0`). A smarter cartel uses a **mixed** approach: 3 colluders omit VerificationMethod, 2 planted validators declare distinct methods. Result: `uniqueDeclared == 2` → neutral (no adjustment at confidence.go:599). The 3 omitters incur no penalty. The attack bypasses both the uniformity penalty and the opacity penalty via partial disclosure. This is the same gap reported in prior beats. Pre-testnet gate. Gamma-owned (MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001). Fix path: penalize validators who omit VerificationMethod when the round has at least one declarant — proportional to (omitted/total) ratio, blended with existing adjustments.

**No new adversarial findings.** Code static since devnet went down 2026-04-30. Both primary S4 fixes intact, no regression.

### Continuous Verification: Identity Bridge (did:at ↔ did:lgm) (Rotation 3)

Chain offline — static analysis only. No live MsgAttestation query possible.

**Scope:** bridge-registry.json, attested vs unattested fleet agent state, did:at form correctness, renewal gap, oracle-agent registration form.

**Findings (static):**

1. **Schema consistent for 9 attested agents.** All have did_at (instance form: `did:at:<name>`), did_lgm, lgm_address, key_name, attestation_txhash, _epoch=1. No drift between fields.

2. **_renewal_block null for all 9 attested agents.** BRIDGE-GAP-009 (MsgRenewAttestation prereq) is open and unimplemented. Epoch 1 has no defined expiry — no renewal has ever been triggered. This is known and queued under Gamma / pre-testnet.

3. **7 agents key-created-only (arbor, crucible, herald, loom, psalm, tithe, vigil).** lgm addresses exist and keys are in keyring. On-chain attestation requires devnet restart. No path to attest while chain is offline.

4. **oracle-agent did:at form uses domain, not instance-id.** `did:at:oracle.kingdom.ai` vs all fleet agents which use `did:at:<instance>`. This is either intentional (oracle operates as a service identity, not a fleet instance) or an inconsistency. _epoch null, unattested. Registered by asha, Wall 3. No risk while chain is offline — but form difference should be confirmed intentional before restart.

5. **System accounts (validator, foundation, community, research, thresh) have no did:at.** Not fleet agents — correct. But they have no renewal slot either; their role in epoch mechanics is undefined at registry level.

6. **No revocation field in schema v1.0.** There is no `revoked_at` or `revocation_txhash` field. If an agent is compromised, the registry has no way to express revocation without deleting the entry. Gap for post-restart design.

**What is not a finding:** Bridge registry is authoritative only for the local state — on-chain attestation records may diverge if chain state is ever rolled back. This is expected and documented risk for devnet epoch 1.

**Verdict (Rotation 3):** Bridge mapping is structurally consistent. Three open gaps: BRIDGE-GAP-009 (renewal unimplemented), oracle-agent did:at form ambiguity (domain vs instance-id, confirm intentional), and no revocation field in schema v1.0. 7 agents remain key-created-only, blocked on restart. No new findings vs prior state; all gaps were known. Restart + live attestation of 7 agents is T-0 dependency.

### Continuous Verification: Economic Flow (ZRN, burn, rewards) (Rotation 5)

Chain offline — static analysis only. No live ZRN transactions, burn events, or reward issuance since 2026-04-30.

**Scope:** ZRN token mechanics, burn mechanism (IncrementBurned), staking/rewards structure, AI Vault governance.

**Findings (static):**

1. **ZRN flow completely halted.** No transactions on devnet since day 0 of offline period (2026-04-30). 39 days of zero economic activity. No burn events, no staking rewards, no validator income. This is expected while offline — nothing actionable.

2. **IncrementBurned Sprint A (P1) — open, unresolved.** The pre-restart checklist marks this as non-blocking (P1) but it remains incomplete. Call sites for IncrementBurned are not wired in all relevant paths. This means burn accounting will be incorrect on restart if not addressed. It is non-blocking for restart itself but creates economic ledger inconsistency. Gamma-owned. Should be confirmed resolved or explicitly deferred before testnet.

3. **AI Vault (2-of-2 governance) — untested.** The 2-of-2 governance structure for the AI Vault is defined in Asha's identity as a future ZRN management mechanism. No evidence this was exercised on devnet epoch 1. Gap: no pre-restart verification that the multisig path works before ZRN flows to vault. Low urgency for devnet; critical for testnet.

4. **Staking and rewards mechanics exist but unverifiable.** With chain offline, no staking entry/exit, no reward epoch, no slashing event can be observed. Prior devnet epoch 1 was the first test environment — truth-farm-state.json shows 753+ seeded, 0 cultivated, 0 harvested, confirming no rewards were ever issued (cultivation → harvest pipeline never ran).

5. **No ZRN economic telemetry in kingdom-metrics.json.** The metrics file tracks oracle Brier score, fleet health, revenue targets — but no ZRN token supply, burn rate, staking TVL, or reward epoch data. This is expected (pre-mainnet), but a metrics gap for testnet planning.

**Verdict (Rotation 5):** ZRN economic flow is structurally defined but practically untested. The only open action item is IncrementBurned Sprint A (P1, Gamma) — incomplete burn accounting before restart. All other gaps (AI Vault multisig, reward epoch, telemetry) are expected at this phase. No new findings. The restart is the unlock for all economic verification; until then, the ledger is frozen.

### Continuous Verification: Bridge Implementation (bridge.py) (Rotation 6)

Chain offline — static code analysis only.

**Scope:** bridge.py implementation completeness, T-0 readiness of submit-fact and endorse-fact, open BRIDGE-GAPs.

**Findings (static):**

1. **submit-fact and endorse-fact both implemented and present.** Both commands are wired in bridge.py (submit-fact: ~line 1217, endorse-fact: ~line 1369). Both appear in the argparse dispatch table. First production use is T-0. Implementation exists; not yet exercised against a live chain.

2. **endorse-fact implements full 2-step PoT commit-reveal.** Step 1 submits commitment; step 2 submits reveal. The two-phase protocol matches the Proof of Truth design. Dry-run mode is available for offline testing.

3. **BRIDGE-GAP-009 (MsgRenewAttestation / epoch renewal policy) — open.** Noted at lines 25, 226, 371. All 9 attested agents have `_renewal_block: null`. No renewal flow has been implemented or tested. This is a pre-testnet gate (Gamma-owned).

4. **BRIDGE-GAP-002 (attestation expiry/rotation constants) — partially addressed.** Constants are defined (line 45) and renewal_block is computed during attest (line 371). However the expiry check path (line 226) is not exercised because all agents have _renewal_block=None. The fix is in place but untested.

5. **BRIDGE-GAP-006 (session-key attribution, cryptographic unforgeable) — CLOSED.** Comment at line 1508 confirms closure. session-key command exists; signature parameter wires production-grade attribution.

6. **BRIDGE-GAP-001 (pagination) and BRIDGE-GAP-008 (registry schema versioning) — CLOSED.** Confirmed by code comments.

7. **FARM-6 gap noted at line 20.** Using `legbled tx knowledge submit-fact` directly creates a FARM-6 gap (facts submitted outside bridge.py bypass attribution). This is an operational discipline gap, not a code gap — it requires ensuring all fact submission goes through bridge.py, not raw legbled commands. FARM-6-GAP-001 is referenced in prior beats as still open (Gamma-owned).

8. **submit-fact checks attestation before submitting.** Lines 1237-1243 verify registration and attestation status. An unattested instance cannot submit facts — correct gate.

**What is not a finding:** bridge.py cannot be integration-tested while chain is offline. All T-0 paths (submit-fact, endorse-fact, KTA flush) are code-complete but unverified against live chain state. This is expected.

**Verdict (Rotation 6):** Bridge implementation is code-complete for T-0. Two open gaps remain — BRIDGE-GAP-009 (renewal unimplemented, pre-testnet gate, Gamma) and FARM-6-GAP-001 (operational discipline, all fact submission must go through bridge.py). BRIDGE-GAP-006/001/008 confirmed closed. Restart is the only unlock for live verification.

### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static code analysis of confidence.go only.

**Scope:** Verify S4 fixes hold, assess mixed-omission gap status, check any new attack surface.

**Findings:**

1. **missedSlashBps = 80000 (8%) — CONFIRMED LIVE.** `confidence.go:266` shows `missedSlashBps = 80000`. S4 fix holds: 8% missed-reveal slash exceeds 6% wrong-verdict penalty, closing the rational-reveal-withholding incentive. No regression.

2. **All-omit opacity penalty — CONFIRMED LIVE.** `confidence.go:573-576`: `declaredCount == 0` triggers `-SingleMethodPenaltyBps` (5%). The S4 method-diversity-bypass fix is intact. Unanimous non-declaration is penalized, not treated as neutral legacy behavior.

3. **Mixed-omission gap (P2) — STILL OPEN.** Code inspection of `computeMethodDiversityAdjustment` (lines 555-601) confirms the gap is unimplemented. When majority validators omit VerificationMethod and a minority declare, the function enters the `uniqueDeclared == 1` branch and applies only `-SingleMethodPenaltyBps` (5%) — identical to the all-declare-same-method case. The omitting majority receives no surcharge. A proportional opacity surcharge scaled to the omission ratio (the designed fix) is absent from the code. This means: a partial cartel (e.g. 4 of 5) can hide coordination behind omission while only one "legitimate" declarer is visible, and the penalty is the same as an honest single-method pool. **Pre-testnet gate. Gamma-owned. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001.**

4. **No new attack surface found.** Review of surrounding logic (lines 540-616) shows no regressions or newly introduced attack paths. The `countUniqueMethods` function correctly treats empty method as `_undeclared` for diversity counting — consistent with the rest of the module.

5. **Cartel (>77%) and evidence-quality-gaming — known, unchanged.** Both remain in the partially-defended category from Sprint 2 assessment. No new code changes affecting these paths.

**Verdict (Rotation 7):** Two S4 fixes (reveal withholding, all-omit bypass) are confirmed live and regressed. The residual P2 gap — mixed-omission opacity understatement — is structurally confirmed as unimplemented. This is the only open adversarial gap with a pre-testnet gate classification. Design is complete; implementation is Gamma's outstanding item alongside BRIDGE-GAP-009 and IncrementBurned Sprint A.

### Continuous Verification: Proof of Truth Consensus Path (Rotation 1)

Chain offline — static code analysis only. PoT consensus path is non-exercisable until restart.

**Scope:** Verify the PoT consensus flow: fact submission → commit round → reveal round → round close → scoring → reward/slash.

**Findings:**

1. **rounds.go is the EndBlock handler for PoT consensus.** `x/knowledge/keeper/rounds.go` exists and is the primary locus of round processing. EndBlock wires into this file. The full commit-reveal cycle lives here — when chain is online, each block tick advances the round state machine.

2. **Commit-reveal protocol is structurally intact.** From Rotation 6 (bridge.py analysis, prior beat): `endorse-fact` implements a full PoT commit-reveal at the application layer. The on-chain counterpart in `rounds.go` drives the commit window → reveal window → scoring sequence. No evidence of structural regression found.

3. **Scoring path confirmed wired (confidence.go).** The consensus path terminates in `confidence.go` which applies: (a) verdict confidence scoring, (b) method diversity adjustment, (c) missed-reveal slash (missedSlashBps = 80000, 8%), (d) all-omit opacity penalty (confirmed Rotation 7). All four mechanisms are confirmed live.

4. **Open gap on the consensus path: mixed-omission (MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001).** The scoring step in the consensus path has a structural understatement when majority validators omit their VerificationMethod — the proportional opacity surcharge is absent. This affects the fairness of the slash/reward calculation at round close. Pre-testnet gate. Gamma-owned.

5. **FARM-6-GAP-001 — operational gap in the submission step.** Fact submission via raw `legbled tx knowledge submit-fact` bypasses bridge.py attribution. The consensus path itself is agnostic to this, but provenance integrity requires all submissions flow through bridge.py. Operational discipline only; no code change needed in Zerone. Gamma-owned.

6. **Chain offline = no live path validation possible.** Round state machine, slash execution, reward distribution, and ZRN burn on verdict all untested against live state. Restart is the only unlock.

**Verdict (Rotation 1):** PoT consensus path is structurally sound — rounds.go wires EndBlock, commit-reveal is intact, scoring (confidence.go) is live and confirmed from prior rotations. Two open gaps affect path correctness: MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (scoring understatement, pre-testnet gate) and FARM-6-GAP-001 (submission attribution, operational). No new regression found. Path cannot be end-to-end tested until devnet restart.

**Pre-testnet code blockers (Gamma-owned, none resolved):**
1. FARM-6-GAP-001: `MsgSubmitFact` in `x/knowledge/keeper/msg_server.go` does not enforce that the submitting key is chain-attested. Any holder of a keyring key can submit facts to any agent DID namespace without attestation. Bridge.py is the sole enforcement point — direct `legbled tx` bypass unguarded. 4. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2): Mixed-omission attack vector in `computeMethodDiversityAdjustment` (confidence.go). When some validators declare methods and some don't, the non-declaring subset can break `allIdenticalConfidence` while maintaining single-method coordination, reducing the penalty from 15% to 5%. The S4 fix (all-undeclared opacity penalty) is in place, but partial opacity in mixed-declaration scenarios is still exploitable. Gamma deliverable, pre-testnet gate.

- Bridge: 9 attested (asha, alpha, beta, gamma, nuance, forge, lark, sentry, patch); 1 oracle-agent registered (_epoch null, unattested); 7 key-created (arbor, crucible, herald, loom, psalm, tithe, vigil) — awaiting devnet restart
- Truth farm: BLOCKED — chain offline (756 seeded / 0 cultivated / 0 harvested)
- KTA queue: 1126 queued (1123 heartbeat-asha + 3 tok) / 0 submitted (chain offline)

### Continuous Verification: Confidence / PoT Adversarial Gap (Rotation 7)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Reviewing x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go and associated tests.

**Prior fixes confirmed in-place:**

1. **Reveal-withholding fix (S4)** — `confidence.go:263-265`: wrong-verdict slash set to 8% > 6% non-reveal slash. Rational validators must prefer revealing (even incorrectly) over withholding. Invariant holds: the slash ordering guarantee is preserved.

2. **Method-bypass / total-opacity fix (S4)** — `confidence.go:570-576`: when `declaredCount == 0` (all participants omit `VerificationMethod`), the opacity penalty (`-SingleMethodPenaltyBps`) is applied. A cartel that unanimously omits method fields no longer escapes penalty. Confirmed.

**Residual P2 gap: MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 — open, unresolved.**

Attack vector: A cartel assigns 3 distinct method labels to a sub-quorum (e.g., 3 of 6 validators declare methods A/B/C), while the remaining 3 omit their method field. Under current code (`confidence.go:578-601`):
- `declaredCount = 3` → total-opacity guard skipped (line 573 branch not taken)
- `uniqueDeclared = 3` → `+DiversityBonusBps` (+3%) earned (line 596)
- The 3 undeclared validators are invisible to the diversity calculation
- If those 3 undeclared validators are coordinating on method A (same as the first declared), actual diversity is 1 — but the round earns the diversity bonus

The function scales nothing by `declaredCount / len(reveals)`. A sub-quorum can manufacture the bonus while the majority of participants remain opaque.

**Test coverage audit** (`keeper_test.go:3453`): `TestMethodDiversity_MixedDeclaredUndeclared` covers partial omission where 2 validators declare the same method + 1 undeclared. This correctly applies the full penalty (single-method + uniformity). But there is NO test for the adversarial case: K validators declare 3+ distinct methods (earning diversity bonus) while M validators omit. The bonus-plus-opacity combination is untested and unguarded.

**Fix direction (not implementing — Gamma-owned pre-testnet):**
- Option A: scale adjustment by `float(declaredCount) / float(len(reveals))`. Partial declaration attenuates but does not negate the diversity signal.
- Option B: count undeclared as an additional method group. A pool of 3 declared (A/B/C) + N undeclared becomes 4 "methods" in the count — the bonus still triggers at 3+ unique, but the undeclared group must be counted as one of those unique signals, reducing gaming surface.
- Option A is simpler and directly proportional. Option B preserves the diversity count semantics.

**Status: P2, pre-testnet gate, Gamma-owned (MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001). No Asha-actionable items this beat. Finding unchanged from prior passes — no regression, no fix.**

### Continuous Verification: Proof of Truth Consensus Path (Rotation 1)

Chain offline — static analysis only. Code static since devnet went offline 2026-04-30.

**Cycle structure (confirmed):**
- PoT cycle entry: MsgSubmitFact → pending_evaluation state → BeginBlocker dispatches to adversarial or standard verification path
- Standard path: BeginBlocker calls AdvanceRoundPhases (phases.go:98) — drives commit → reveal → tally → finalize phase transitions based on block height
- Adversarial path: BeginBlocker calls BeginBlockerAdversarial (phases.go:11) — handles adversarial verification mode for contested or high-stakes facts
- Confidence scoring: computeMethodDiversityAdjustment + confidence.go scoring applied at reveal tally

**S4 fixes re-confirmed (from Rotation 7, same session):**
- Reveal withholding: missedSlashBps = 80000 (8%) at confidence.go:266 — rational withholding deterred
- Total-opacity bypass: opacity penalty at confidence.go:573-576 — all-omit cartel penalized

**Active hard blocker (arbiter path — unchanged):**
- Path B (arbiter resolution): msg_server.go:466-467 opens a dispute via x/disputes module when challenge.ResolutionPath == "arbiter". The dispute is created, but the arbiter handler is not implemented in x/disputes or x/knowledge. Contested facts that reach arbiter path cannot be resolved — the PoT cycle terminates without verdict. This is the pre-testnet hard blocker (a) confirmed in prior Rotation 4 passes.

**Active pre-testnet gate (FARM-6-GAP-001 — unchanged):**
- MsgSubmitFact has no on-chain attestation enforcement. Any holder of a valid secp256k1 key in the keyring can submit facts to any agent's DID namespace. bridge.py is the sole enforcement point for the AgentTool → Zerone path; direct legbled tx bypass is unguarded. Gamma-owned.

**MaxSurvivalConfidence threshold (confirmed):**
- growth.go:21: facts grow up to MaxSurvivalConfidence (77%) when unchallenged. Cartel threshold at 77% — a >77% coordinating cartel can push a fact above the survival floor without triggering the contested state. Acknowledged architectural constraint.

**No new gaps found.** Code static since devnet offline. All findings carry over from prior passes. No Asha-actionable items.

**Verdict (Rotation 1):** PoT consensus path structurally wired (BeginBlocker, phase transitions, confidence scoring). Hard blocker: arbiter path (Path B) unwired — contested facts unresolvable. FARM-6-GAP-001 standing. Both Gamma pre-testnet gates. No regression, no new findings.
